EP5 龐貝：分鏡表

D 分鏡（第 13～17 步）：分鏡模型拆鏡頭 → 檢查 → 補背景和參考圖 → Flow 畫圖 → 挑選用條、下載、配音、壓粗剪。畫圖之前這份報告就有內容（只是缺圖），可以先看；流程只在粗剪停下來。

第 1 場　博士的實驗室（研究小屋一樓）　4 個鏡頭＋0 個遠景備份

檢查通過：旁白全部分到鏡頭、一字不差、順序對；遠景備份齊全；欄位都對。

這場的地圖（lab）

EP5-01-01

你一推開實驗室的門，

景別・方位・角度：遠景（WS）・正面・平視

主體：HERO　動作：HERO 一手推開木門、跨步進屋的瞬間，還沒發現異狀。　畫面方向：toward

入鏡：HERO（全身，約 5 成）

構圖：HERO 置中略偏下，站在畫面中央的門口，正一手推門、跨步進來；四周是實驗室空間，博士不入鏡。

地圖上：相機 L12、高 1.6 m、對準 HERO

參考圖：REF_HERO_SUMMER、LAB_DOOR_F

完整 prompt（中文）
16:9，生成 1 張圖。完全照所附角色設定表的風格畫平面 2D 卡通：粗黑輪廓線、平塗色塊。畫面中任何地方都不可以有文字。畫面中只有 HERO。HERO（完全依照所附的 HERO 角色設定表），夏裝，沒戴帽子也沒背背包，正推開一扇木門往前邁步，一隻手還搭在門邊，另一隻手臂前後擺動。他的表情輕鬆、帶點好奇，毫無警覺，直直往前看。HERO 的臉和手是純紙白色。景別：Wide Shot (WS)。HERO 全身清楚入鏡，周圍有大量環境。鏡頭方向：正面視角。鏡頭在 HERO 前方的房間裡，面對著他。鏡頭角度：Eye-level：鏡頭位在 HERO 的眼睛高度，平視前方。　對著有大門的那面木板牆。牆中間是研究小屋的木頭大門，門旁邊（畫面左側）有一扇小窗，窗外是校園的草地和樹。斑駁的木板牆、舊木頭地板。白天，早上的陽光從畫面左側那扇小窗照進來，頭頂垂著幾顆燈泡。
英文原文（送 Flow 的）

16:9, generate 1 image. Flat 2D cartoon in exactly the style of the attached character sheets: thick black outlines, flat colors. No text anywhere in the picture. Only HERO is in the picture. HERO (exactly as in the attached HERO character sheet), summer outfit, no hat and no backpack, is pushing open a wooden door and stepping forward mid-stride, one hand still on the door edge, the other arm swinging. His face is relaxed and a little curious, unaware of anything, looking straight ahead. HERO's face and hands are pure paper white. Shot size: Wide Shot (WS). HERO's whole body is clearly visible, with a lot of the surroundings around him. Camera direction: front view. The camera is in the room ahead of HERO, facing him. Camera angle: Eye-level: the camera is at HERO's eye height, looking straight ahead. Background: Facing the wooden plank wall with the front door. In the middle of the wall is the hut's wooden front door; beside it, on the left of the frame, a small window shows campus lawn and trees outside. Weathered wooden plank wall and an old wooden plank floor. Daytime; morning light comes in through the small window on the left, with a few bare bulbs hanging overhead.

第 17 步還沒做

EP5-01-02

（停）就被噴了一臉。

景別・方位・角度：特寫（CU）・正面・平視

主體：HERO 的臉　動作：停頓一拍後，泡泡「噗」地噴上 HERO 的臉，他往後一縮、瞇眼、死魚臉。　畫面方向：toward

入鏡：HERO（胸口以上、臉和雙手，約 8.5 成）

構圖：HERO 的臉佔畫面中央，白泡泡從畫面右側噴濺上臉、往下滴，幾顆泡泡還懸在空中；背景簡單。

地圖上：相機 L17、高 1.6 m、對準 HERO

參考圖：REF_HERO_SUMMER、LAB_DOOR_F

完整 prompt（中文）
16:9，生成 1 張圖。完全照所附角色設定表的風格畫平面 2D 卡通：粗黑輪廓線、平塗色塊。畫面中任何地方都不可以有文字。畫面中只有 HERO。HERO（完全依照所附的 HERO 角色設定表），夏裝，沒戴帽子也沒背背包，臉上剛被一陣白色肥皂泡沫噴個正著；一團團白泡沫黏在他臉上並往下滴，面前空中還飄著幾顆泡泡。他正往後縮，雙眼緊閉，嘴抿成一條直線，面無表情、徹底無言。HERO 的臉和手是純紙白色。景別：Medium Close-up (MCU)。HERO 取胸部以上入鏡，畫面下緣落在他的胸口。鏡頭方向：正面視角。鏡頭在 HERO 前方的房間裡，面對著他。鏡頭角度：Eye-level：鏡頭位在 HERO 的眼睛高度，平視前方。　HERO 後面緊貼著小屋的木頭大門，木板門面佔滿畫面。門旁小窗的一角透進白天的光，從畫面左側照過來。斑駁的舊木頭牆面。
英文原文（送 Flow 的）

16:9, generate 1 image. Flat 2D cartoon in exactly the style of the attached character sheets: thick black outlines, flat colors. No text anywhere in the picture. Only HERO is in the picture. HERO (exactly as in the attached HERO character sheet), summer outfit, no hat and no backpack, has just been sprayed in the face with a burst of white soapy foam and bubbles; blobs of white foam cling to his face and drip down, a few bubbles still in the air in front of him. He is flinching backward, eyes scrunched, mouth a flat line, deadpan and thoroughly unimpressed. HERO's face and hands are pure paper white. Shot size: Medium Close-up (MCU). HERO is framed from the chest up; the bottom edge of the picture is at his chest. Camera direction: front view. The camera is in the room ahead of HERO, facing him. Camera angle: Eye-level: the camera is at HERO's eye height, looking straight ahead. Background: Right behind HERO is the hut's wooden front door, its plank surface filling the frame. A corner of the small window beside it lets in daylight from the left. Weathered old wooden wall.

第 17 步還沒做

EP5-01-03

原來博士正在做火山模型，小蘇打加醋，噴得滿屋子都是泡泡。

景別・方位・角度：遠景（WS）・側面・平視

主體：PROF（和噴發的火山模型）　動作：火山模型正噴出泡泡的瞬間，PROF 俯身在旁、滿臉得意。　畫面方向：right

入鏡：PROF（全身（側身），約 5 成）

構圖：PROF 在畫面中央側身，畫面右手邊是正在噴發的火山模型，白泡泡四處飛濺、幾乎充滿整個畫面；呈現滿屋泡泡。

地圖上：相機 T13、高 1.4 m、對準 PROF

參考圖：REF_PROF、LAB_CHALK_F

完整 prompt（中文）
16:9，生成 1 張圖。完全照所附角色設定表的風格畫平面 2D 卡通：粗黑輪廓線、平塗色塊。畫面中任何地方都不可以有文字。畫面中只有 PROF。PROF（完全依照所附的 PROF 角色設定表）正俯身看著一座自製的玩具火山模型噴發，一道高高的白色小蘇打加醋泡沫從頂端噴湧而出，泡泡在他四周到處飛。他露出得意又開心的大大笑容，雙眼興奮睜大，顯然對自己的實驗得意極了。景別：Wide Shot (WS)。PROF 全身清楚入鏡，周圍有大量環境。鏡頭方向：側面視角。鏡頭在側邊，完全從側面看著 PROF。鏡頭角度：Eye-level：鏡頭位在 PROF 的眼睛高度，平視前方。　對著房間另一頭的木板牆，牆上是一大塊綠色黑板。黑板在 PROF 後方的牆上，中間隔著一大片舊木頭地板。斑駁的木板牆。白天，早上的陽光從鏡頭後方照過來，頭頂垂著燈泡。
英文原文（送 Flow 的）

16:9, generate 1 image. Flat 2D cartoon in exactly the style of the attached character sheets: thick black outlines, flat colors. No text anywhere in the picture. Only PROF is in the picture. PROF (exactly as in the attached PROF character sheet) is leaning over a homemade toy volcano model that is erupting, a tall fountain of white baking-soda-and-vinegar foam gushing out of its top and bubbles flying all around him. He has a delighted, proud grin, eyes wide with glee, clearly thrilled with his experiment. Shot size: Wide Shot (WS). PROF's whole body is clearly visible, with a lot of the surroundings around him. Camera direction: side view. The camera is to the side and sees PROF exactly from the side. Camera angle: Eye-level: the camera is at PROF's eye height, looking straight ahead. Background: Facing the far wooden plank wall, which holds a big green chalkboard. The chalkboard is on the wall behind PROF, with a wide stretch of old wooden floor between. Weathered wooden plank walls. Daytime; morning light comes from behind the camera, with bare bulbs hanging overhead.

第 17 步還沒做

EP5-01-04

他很得意，你很不得意。

景別・方位・角度：遠景（WS）・側面・平視

主體：HERO 和 PROF　動作：HERO 滴著泡泡、面無表情地瞪著；PROF 挺胸得意地笑，形成強烈對比。　畫面方向：right

入鏡：HERO（全身，約 5 成）；PROF（全身，約 5 成）

構圖：HERO 在畫面左、面向右；PROF 在畫面右、面向左；兩人對望形成反差。PROF 身後（畫面右）隱約是噴過的火山模型與殘留泡泡。

地圖上：相機 O17、高 1.5 m、對準 HERO 和 PROF

參考圖：REF_HERO_SUMMER、REF_PROF、LAB_MACHINE_F、LAB_MACHINE_L、LAB_MACHINE_R

完整 prompt（中文）
16:9，生成 1 張圖。完全照所附角色設定表的風格畫平面 2D 卡通：粗黑輪廓線、平塗色塊。畫面中任何地方都不可以有文字。畫面中只有 HERO 和 PROF。左邊，HERO（完全依照所附的 HERO 角色設定表），夏裝，沒戴帽子也沒背背包，臉上和身上還濺著、滴著白色泡沫，僵硬地面朝右邊的 PROF 站著，一臉面無表情、徹底無言，瞪著他。右邊，PROF（完全依照所附的 PROF 角色設定表）面朝左邊的 HERO，挺起胸膛，咧著超得意的大大笑容，一隻手得意地指著自己。HERO 的臉和手是純紙白色。景別：Wide Shot (WS)。兩個角色全身都清楚入鏡，周圍有大量環境。鏡頭方向：側面視角。鏡頭在側邊，完全從側面看著 HERO 和 PROF。鏡頭角度：Eye-level：鏡頭位在 HERO 的眼睛高度，平視前方。　對著有時光機的那面木板牆。牆中間是圓環狀的大型金屬時光機，粗電纜從它拖過地板、伸向兩人這邊，圓環右邊的木箱上有一支紅色大拉桿；畫面左側的牆上掛滿大大小小、各不相同的時鐘，時鐘下方的小桌上是用管子、齒輪和一個凹掉的水壺做成的自製咖啡機；畫面右側的角落是一整面轉盤、開關、指示燈加上幾台舊 CRT 螢幕的控制台。舊木頭地板。白天，早上的陽光從畫面右側的窗戶照進來，頭頂垂著燈泡。
英文原文（送 Flow 的）

16:9, generate 1 image. Flat 2D cartoon in exactly the style of the attached character sheets: thick black outlines, flat colors. No text anywhere in the picture. Only HERO and PROF are in the picture. On the left, HERO (exactly as in the attached HERO character sheet), summer outfit, no hat and no backpack, his face and body still spattered and dripping with white foam, stands stiffly facing right toward PROF, flat deadpan expression, thoroughly unimpressed, glaring. On the right, PROF (exactly as in the attached PROF character sheet) faces left toward HERO, chest puffed out, beaming an enormous proud grin, one hand gesturing proudly at himself. HERO's face and hands are pure paper white. Shot size: Wide Shot (WS). Both characters' whole bodies are clearly visible, with a lot of the surroundings around them. Camera direction: side view. The camera is to the side and sees HERO and PROF exactly from the side. Camera angle: Eye-level: the camera is at HERO's eye height, looking straight ahead. Background: Facing the wooden plank wall with the time machine. In the middle stands the big ring-shaped metal time machine, with thick cables snaking across the floor from it toward the two; on a wooden box to the right of the ring is a big red lever. On the wall to the left hang dozens of mismatched clocks, and on a small table below them sits a homemade coffee machine of tubes, gears and a dented kettle. In the right-hand corner stands a control console — a wall of dials, switches, blinking lights and a few old CRT monitors. Old wooden plank floor. Daytime; morning light comes in from the window on the right, with bare bulbs hanging overhead.

第 17 步還沒做

第 2 場　博士的實驗室（研究小屋一樓）　8 個鏡頭＋0 個遠景備份

檢查通過：旁白全部分到鏡頭、一字不差、順序對；遠景備份齊全；欄位都對。

這場的地圖（lab）

EP5-02-01

你拿出一本小冊子，上面記著博士欠你的錢，一頁比一頁長。

景別・方位・角度：中遠景（MLS）・側面・平視

主體：HERO 與 PROF（雙人）　動作：HERO 一手把越記越長的欠薪本遞向博士、一手攤開討錢，滿臉期待；博士抱胸無視。　畫面方向：left

入鏡：HERO（膝蓋以上全身、臉、雙手、欠薪本，約 78%）；PROF（膝蓋以上全身、臉、雙手（抱胸），約 76%）

構圖：博士在畫面左（西）、HERO 在畫面右（東），兩人側面相對；HERO 伸出的欠薪本在兩人之間、攤開展成長長一條；背景是西側黑板牆一帶。

地圖上：相機 H16、高 1.5 m、對準 HERO 與 PROF 之間

參考圖：REF_HERO_SUMMER、REF_PROF、LAB_MACHINE_L、LAB_CHALK_R

完整 prompt（中文）
16:9，生成 1 張圖。完全依照所附角色設定圖的風格：扁平 2D 卡通，粗黑輪廓線、平塗色塊。畫面任何地方都不能有文字。畫面中只有兩個角色：HERO（完全照所附 HERO 角色設定圖，穿夏季服裝，不戴帽子、不背背包）和 PROF（完全照所附 PROF 角色設定圖）。HERO 的臉和手是純紙白色。HERO 站在右邊，伸出一本小筆記本，攤開的那一頁展開成一長條正字記號（只有記號，沒有文字、沒有數字）朝向 PROF，另一隻手張開、空著，像在要錢；他一臉充滿希望、懇求的樣子，眉毛挑高，帶著一絲期待的微笑。PROF 站在左邊，面向 HERO 雙手抱胸，無視那本筆記本，面無表情、一臉不以為然，無聊地微微皺眉。Shot size：Medium Long Shot (MLS)。HERO 從膝蓋以上入鏡；畫面下緣落在他的膝蓋。Camera direction：側視。鏡頭在他們旁邊，完全從側面看 HERO 和 PROF。Camera angle：Eye-level，鏡頭在 HERO 的眼睛高度，平視向前。　對著掛滿時鐘的那面牆。牆上掛滿大大小小的時鐘，時鐘下方的小桌上是管子齒輪拼成、水壺凹一塊的自製咖啡機。往右是金屬圓環的時光機，粗電纜從它底下拖到地上。畫面最左邊是牆角，轉過去就是擺綠色黑板的那面牆。地上是老舊的木地板。晨光從右邊的窗那側灑進來，天花板垂著亮著的燈泡。
英文原文（送 Flow 的）

16:9, generate 1 image. Flat 2D cartoon in exactly the style of the attached character sheets: thick black outlines, flat colors. No text anywhere in the picture. Only two characters in the picture: HERO (exactly as in the attached HERO character sheet, in his summer outfit, no hat, no backpack) and PROF (exactly as in the attached PROF character sheet). HERO's face and hands are pure paper white. HERO stands on the right, holding out a small notebook whose page unfolds into a long strip of tally marks (marks only, no text, no numbers) toward PROF, his other hand open and empty asking for money; he looks hopeful and pleading, eyebrows raised, a small expectant smile. PROF stands on the left facing HERO with his arms crossed, ignoring the notebook, flat and unimpressed, a bored half-frown. Shot size: Medium Long Shot (MLS). HERO is framed from the knees up; the bottom edge of the picture is at his knees. Camera direction: side view. The camera is beside them and sees HERO and PROF exactly from the side. Camera angle: Eye-level: the camera is at HERO's eye height, looking straight ahead. Background: Facing the clock-covered wall. The wall is covered with dozens of mismatched clocks, and under them a small table holds a rickety homemade coffee machine of tubes, gears and a dented kettle. To the right stands the ring-shaped metal time machine with thick cables snaking across the floor from it. At the far left is the corner turning to the wall with the green chalkboard. The floor is an old wooden plank floor. Morning light spills in from the windows on the right, and lit bulbs hang from the ceiling.

第 17 步還沒做

EP5-02-02

博士看都沒看，就把一個空玻璃罐塞進你手裡。

景別・方位・角度：中景（MS）・前方斜側・平視

主體：PROF（越肩看向博士）　動作：博士眼睛看別處、一臉無所謂，手卻把空玻璃罐硬塞進 HERO 攤開的手掌。　畫面方向：off_down

入鏡：PROF（腰部以上、臉、雙手、玻璃罐，約 70%）；HERO（背對鏡頭的肩膀與一隻伸出的手（臉不入鏡），約 45%）

構圖：前景右下是 HERO 的肩膀與伸出的一隻手（背對鏡頭）；畫面中後是博士，頭轉開、把空玻璃罐塞進 HERO 手裡。

地圖上：相機 M10、高 1.5 m、對準 PROF

參考圖：REF_HERO_SUMMER、REF_PROF、LAB_CHALK_R、LAB_MACHINE_L

完整 prompt（中文）
16:9，生成 1 張圖。完全依照所附角色設定圖的風格：扁平 2D 卡通，粗黑輪廓線、平塗色塊。畫面任何地方都不能有文字。畫面中只有兩個角色：PROF（完全照所附 PROF 角色設定圖）和 HERO（完全照所附 HERO 角色設定圖，夏季服裝，不戴帽子、不背背包），HERO 只以靠近鏡頭的前景肩膀和一隻在右下方伸出、張開的手出現，背對我們，所以看不到他的臉。HERO 的手是純紙白色。我們越過 HERO 的肩膀看向 PROF。PROF 把一個空玻璃罐塞進 HERO 張開的手裡，看都不看他一眼，頭轉向一邊，面無表情、一臉無聊呆滯，眼睛望向別處、往下看。Shot size：Medium Shot (MS)。PROF 從腰部以上入鏡；畫面下緣落在他的腰部。Camera direction：前四分之三視角。鏡頭在 PROF 前方偏一側，因此看得到他的臉和身體的一側。Camera angle：Eye-level，鏡頭在 PROF 的眼睛高度，平視向前。　畫面對著黑板那面牆靠時鐘牆角的一端。這一端的牆上貼著一張電影海報，海報左邊、往畫面左下是那塊綠色大黑板；海報右邊就是牆角，轉過去是掛滿時鐘的那面牆。地上是老舊的木地板。晨光從鏡頭後方照在牆上，天花板垂著亮著的燈泡。
英文原文（送 Flow 的）

16:9, generate 1 image. Flat 2D cartoon in exactly the style of the attached character sheets: thick black outlines, flat colors. No text anywhere in the picture. Only two characters in the picture: PROF (exactly as in the attached PROF character sheet) and HERO (exactly as in the attached HERO character sheet, summer outfit, no hat, no backpack), who is seen only as a close foreground shoulder and one outstretched open hand in the lower right, his back to us so his face is not visible. HERO's hands are pure paper white. We are looking over HERO's shoulder at PROF. PROF shoves an empty glass jar into HERO's open hand without even looking at him, his head turned away, flat deadpan bored expression, eyes looking off and down. Shot size: Medium Shot (MS). Camera angle: High angle: the camera is high above the ledger, looking down at it. Background: the camera looks straight down, so the frame is almost all old wooden plank floor; no wall shows. Morning sunlight slants across the floor from a window behind the camera, plus the ceiling bulbs.
